# ZYRA — Onboarding Preview Lab Handover

**Version:** V1  
**Status:** CURRENT_VERSION — each signup role requires one first photo before Membership (§109–110), preserving Family comparison (§108), guided answer cards (§107), account-handoff artwork (§106), checkpoint motion (§105), four-slide protected opening (§103–104) and approved copy. Canonical account essentials → first photo → Membership → mandatory identity → confirmed profile/photo finalization → explicit profile-now / app-now choice → resumable role setup → confirmed publication. Full Seeker profile still requires three photos. Guardian first-photo client and same-device recovery are checked, installed and native-launched; physical Founder acceptance, backend atomicity, commerce and older visual baselines remain open gates. Earlier dated evidence is history.  
**Date:** 2026-10-12  
**Owner:** Codex under Founder authority / AD-AIOS-111

Earlier instructions and local deliveries remain dated history.

## 1. Exact request and implementation boundary

Founder requested ten Mobbin-inspired onboarding directions while explicitly
preserving the current splash, ZYRA colours and fonts. Founder also asked
which device permissions are necessary. The current launch splash remains
Quiet Horizon with the new nominal 3050ms (2400ms motion + 650ms hold)
source contract and canonical wordmark/tagline. The 3650ms and 4400ms
timings in dated sections below describe earlier local builds.
At the initial lab delivery, no splash, AuthGate, signup question flow,
account state or entitlement was replaced. This was a visual welcome
comparison, not a new registration flow. The subsequent developer Sign up
entry correction is recorded separately in §7; no comparison is adopted as
the production onboarding default.

The Seeker onboarding Summary is Approved rather than literally Finalised,
and retains historical/open dependency references. Guardian onboarding has
separate unresolved design work. This one-off direct Founder instruction
authorises visual studies only; it does not finalise those dependencies,
reconcile question counts by invention, or adopt a new live Guardian flow.
Current Seeker onboarding is labelled explicitly so a Guardian does not
mistake the comparator's Seeker roadmap for their own signup requirements.

The shared Home tools have a separate **Onboarding directions** category
between Splash directions and App icons. Entry, Seeker and Guardian hosts
inherit it. There are **11 choices**: Current Seeker onboarding plus the ten
requested alternatives. Each new direction has three introductory slides:
purpose, private values/preferences, and trusted Guardian support. Current
reuses the existing welcome/profile-stage intro, not the live question flow.

Each preview opens above the invoking route. Close or Finish preview returns
there without login, logout, refresh, reset, draft persistence, completion
writes or real permission prompts. Design arrows cycle through all 11 and
restart only the local visual pages. New directions support horizontal
swiping, Back, Continue, page dots and reduced-motion immediate page changes.
Current retains its existing vertical welcome/stage gestures and 420ms
motion; reduced-motion claims apply to the ten new walkthroughs only.

The picker and already-open lab enforce developer capability. Revocation
removes the artwork/actions and leaves a close-only route. Existing
default-OFF public build and pinned-Founder/protected-role gates remain
unchanged. No new tester grant or TestFlight upload authority is created.

## 2. Reference inspection and original adaptation

The connected Mobbin plugin was verified using the plugin-management skill.
Actual matching iOS screens/flows were inspected, including low-resolution
visual references. Mobbin flows can include splash, authentication and
setup together. These studies adapt the welcome/setup compositions, not
their launch screens or account forms. Search results for ABY under
Structured and Mindvalley under Mozi were rejected as mismatches. Wise/Uber
business-setup results were replaced by matching welcome/safety screens.

| Direction | Adapted visual idea | Inspected matching reference |
|---|---|---|
| Structured | Clear step-by-step hierarchy, timeline and quiet progress | [Structured](https://mobbin.com/screens/04df28b7-0008-4a82-87b8-68ff0f4d0e87) |
| Luma | Centred glow/star composition and rounded primary action | [Luma](https://mobbin.com/flows/9f730ef5-83c9-4478-9440-df07b3b0c66f) |
| Revolut | Confident headline with layered premium cards | [Revolut](https://mobbin.com/flows/7291bae9-7b5a-4b40-a911-316447f4437c) |
| Headspace | Calm organic landscape and reassuring hierarchy | [Headspace](https://mobbin.com/flows/31b21791-dec6-448a-8253-648f5ebbba3e) |
| Mozi | People/circle cards and a centred introductory text pane | [Mozi](https://mobbin.com/screens/a1a812de-79d9-446b-bd8c-d2ad43ecd76a) |
| Wise | Action-oriented clarity, a simple path and role cues | [Wise](https://mobbin.com/screens/6d1da650-099b-49e3-9d36-548586081ca3) |
| Uber | Bold directional hierarchy and illustrated trust/shield | [Uber welcome](https://mobbin.com/screens/2a19fc13-3aec-44e5-b6c4-dc0497bdaf43), [safety](https://mobbin.com/screens/55c1248f-0401-4a0f-970d-21b4a4ba7f9a) |
| Ahead | Friendly central motif, gentle orbit and supportive intent | [Ahead](https://mobbin.com/flows/97103053-f137-4dcd-85af-31f82e7c1e66) |
| How We Feel | Expressive geometric shapes and editorial emphasis | [How We Feel](https://mobbin.com/flows/95dc221a-5b99-4686-99a7-2a638cce8326) |
| Atoms | Restrained dotted grid, orbital progress and small steps | [Atoms](https://mobbin.com/flows/e66e43ab-81b3-4b95-adb2-3d5855f5674c) |

All illustrations are original Flutter/Canvas geometry using the existing
ZYRA theme APIs. No source logos, people photos, copyrighted screenshot
assets or expiring media URLs were downloaded/copied into the app. Poppins
reading type and Syne ZYRA wordmarks remain governed by the current font APIs.
The deep-plum brand gradient and gold accents are unchanged. Decorative
illustrations are excluded from accessibility semantics. Role cues are
illustration, not live role-selection inputs.

## 3. Responsive layout and internal review findings

New walkthroughs keep the footer and comparison controls fixed. Story content
can scroll vertically on short screens or enlarged text; this is not a
whole-page no-scroll claim. Close, arrows, Continue, page dots and Back use
at least44×44 targets. The initial source review repaired36px-wide dot
targets and an inner card wordmark using the reading font; all depicted ZYRA
wordmarks now use Syne. These repairs are owned by OA-ZYRA-257.

The first compact/large-text run exposed the existing comparator's welcome
overflow and fixed49px roadmap rows. The pure intro widget now has an
explicit **allowPreviewScrolling**, defaultfalse. Only the lab opts in:
preview welcome content scrolls, with intrinsic roadmap row heights. Live
signup callers retain their original presentation, copy, callbacks and
default layout. This is a preview accommodation, not a claimed repair of
every legacy live onboarding large-text issue; that retained debt stays
OA-ZYRA-257. The existing live screen still needs its own broader
accessibility review before a live onboarding redesign is adopted.

Entry/Seeker panel tests initially assumed that closing a panel unmounted
its header. Those hosts retain the off-screen animated panel tree. The
tests now check the actual public rail-open state and hit-test invisibility,
while preserving route/session assertions. No host code was changed to make
the tests pass. Independent read-only review covered capability, revocation,
route retention, side effects, factual claims and all ten first-slide real-font
candidates. It is internal evidence, not Founder approval or acceptance.

## 4. Device permission audit — findings, not new prompts

Current code/native declarations were reviewed; no permission transport,
declaration or privacy setting was changed by this work.

| Capability | Current finding and correct timing | Existing ownership |
|---|---|---|
| Location | City is entered manually. No GPS request or continuous/background need. A future optional Use my city shortcut would require a separately approved, one-shot foreground approximate-location path, retaining manual entry. | OA-ZYRA-257 for stale design residue; no new feature approved |
| Notifications | In-app updates exist, but real APNs/FCM permission/token/transport is not connected; developer examples simulate it. Existing policy allows an explainer after the first qualifying Active Journey (Seeker) or accepted link (Guardian), on the next calm landing; prompt only after Enable notifications, never during onboarding. | OA-ZYRA-254 / OA-ZYRA-256 |
| Tracking/ATT | No ATT request/framework, advertising identifier or cross-company tracking integration found. Do not add an unnecessary Allow tracking prompt. Re-audit any future tracking/advertising SDK. | OA-ZYRA-256 for production telemetry/privacy gates |
| Microphone | No calls, voice recording/messages or microphone request found. No need to ask. OS keyboard dictation is not a new app microphone feature. Any future ID-verification SDK must be separately audited. | OA-ZYRA-266 for future IDV provider |
| Camera | Two video-only QR scanners; access only after Scan code, retaining manual code entry if unavailable/denied. | Existing bounded feature; no change |
| Photos | Add photos invokes the native selected-photo picker with full metadata disabled; no broad library/storage permission step should be added to the welcome tour. | OA-ZYRA-302 for remaining image metadata/privacy work |
| Face ID/biometrics | Optional App Lock; request/use only when explicitly enabled or used, with device passcode fallback. | Existing bounded feature; no change |
| Contacts/calendar/Bluetooth/local network | No current feature found that justifies requesting them. | No change authorised |

## 7. 2026-09-28 Sign up entry and in-flow toggle correction

**Dated delivery snapshot:** §8 below supersedes this section's retained
Seeker/Guardian landing shortcuts and its landing/test source hashes. Other
signup isolation/flow behavior and the historical measurements below remain.

### Exact request and as-built behavior

Founder reported an extra developer-only Seeker/Guardian picker after
pressing Sign up, and could not find the new onboarding designs in that
flow's toggle. The landing retains its existing Seeker and Guardian sample
shell shortcuts. **Sign up is now one neutral entry**, without that extra
role sheet. It rehearses the actual Welcome → account role choice → initial
Seeker introduction/questions or Guardian overview/questions → initial
identity-check presentation → local completion screens. Apple/Google actions
are simulated only in this explicitly requested developer preview; normal
production OAuth and signup behavior remain in their default branches.

During signup, the right-hand sliders/Actions toggle presents a separate
**Onboarding directions** category first, containing all11 choices. Existing
Home access remains. Opening a design closes the panel and pushes the visual
lab above the invoking signup route; closing it returns to the current setup
step. This does not insert a selected design into production registration,
choose a live onboarding default, or replace Quiet Horizon.

A fresh protected developer-authority check precedes local isolation and
the existing in-memory sample reset. A nested Navigator contains first-use
replacement/removal routes; a persistent Close header returns to the outer
invoking page. Preview routes disable transition snapshotting and are clipped
below that header. The isolation latch is deliberately retained after Close
or revocation, not released into a real signed-in data path. Signup rehearsal
can change **sample AppState in memory**; it is not a snapshot/restore of all
sample fields. It does not save signup drafts/profile/account changes, sign
out, invoke real OAuth, or write durable member preferences.

Requested-preview flags are now independent of developer capability in
Welcome, role selection, both initial question flows and initial IDV. Denial
or revocation cannot reinterpret an already-requested preview as live signup.
Owner capture, draft writes, consent/provider/finalization/account-exit actions
are guarded; stale callbacks fail closed. Guardian overview's stale Continue
also fails closed; the outer host handles its revocation repaint. A disposed
question-flow parent no longer blocks the permitted local IDV continuation:
the mounted verification-route context and current capability are checked.

Guardian completion reaches a pure local code-orientation screen using a
clearly fictional, non-live-format code. Copy, Share, Scan and Enter explain
their meaning locally; they do not touch clipboard contents, invoke sharing
or camera, compose a real request, or claim one was sent. QR changes local
presentation only. Defaultfalse keeps the existing real code/scanner/link
behavior unchanged. Preview-only generic tap sounds are suppressed; normal
actions retain their prior feedback.

The surface coordinator now keeps ordered identity-based owner registrations.
Disposing a nested completion shell restores an earlier still-mounted owner's
original callbacks; a detached owner is not resurrected. Entry Actions still
provides the neutral Sign up control and all11 design choices throughout.

The Supabase skill informed the deny-first isolation and session/draft review.
Verification used mocked transport, account-bound draft fixtures and durable
preference snapshots, not live member/API/database writes. No SQL, migration,
SDK upgrade, provider/Auth configuration, native permission/signing/trust
change, payment, entitlement, git or TestFlight operation was performed.

### Internal verification and retained findings — OA-ZYRA-257

Final six-suite initial-flow/toggle/coordinator/code run: **94 PASS /0 FAIL**.
Fifteen existing signup, recovery, identity, draft, gender, developer-access,
preview-client and visual-lab regression suites: **174 PASS /1 existing
policy-gated SKIP /0 FAIL**. Seven coordinator cases overlap these groups;
the counts are not represented as268 unique tests. Existing-account IDV
enforcement was not enabled to remove the policy skip. Final capture-only
flow rerun:42 PASS; final six-suite rerun also94 PASS after adding raw captured
Close-glyph pixel assertions to all seven candidates. Final analyzer22 files
PASS/no issues and formatter22/0 changes after that fixture validation.

New initial-flow coverage checks both simulated providers/roles, actual
questions, denied direct construction, revocation and stale callbacks,
zero underlying mocked API/draft operations and unchanged durable member
preferences, local completion, nested-route containment, callback-owner

## 10. 2026-09-29 continuous Onboarding journey

### Founder request and exact boundary

Founder supplied the Structured and Welcome screenshots and instructed:
rename the developer Sign up entry to **Onboarding**, remove
“Sign-up preview · no account changes”, and make every toggle choice start
at the same Apple/Google Welcome page and proceed through the chosen
introduction and existing initial setup/completion sequence. Founder also
explicitly requested the local app update. This direct instruction authorises
the scoped UI implementation and existing-profile local delivery, not real
account creation, backend mutation, production redesign adoption or TestFlight.

This section supersedes the earlier standalone-artwork routing and diagnostic
header in dated §§1–9. Historical files, receipts and visual candidates remain
preserved. Existing OA-ZYRA-257 owns the UI findings and Founder acceptance;
OA-ZYRA-286 owns the one-app delivery boundary. No new identifier or closure.

### As-built sequence and restart ownership

The landing remains **Seeker / Guardian / Onboarding**. The shared Home
Onboarding category still has Current plus ten directions. Selecting a
direction closes the panel and starts the complete local initial journey,
not the old standalone comparison route. If already inside that journey,
the coordinator invokes the most recently attached identity-owned restart
callback. The outer route stays; the nested Navigator is replaced with a
new key, the sample state resets, and the generation increments.

Every direction begins at the existing **Welcome** screen with Apple/Google
buttons. For the ten alternatives, either simulated provider continues into
the chosen three-slide introduction, then the existing role choice and
initial questions/local identity/completion. Current retains the actual
role-dependent original introduction after role choice, so the Seeker
roadmap is not inserted before a Guardian chooses their role. Both role
paths remain available for all eleven styles. The neutral Onboarding action
also restarts an active journey at Welcome.

The diagnostic header text and standalone “Preview · style” controls are
absent from the continuous route; the persistent Close action remains.
The old standalone lab is retained only as an internal visual fixture and
has no runtime launch caller. Intro completion checks developer capability,
process generation, mounted introduction context and current route before
replacing it with role choice. Old or already-completed callbacks cannot
reopen setup after a restart, completion, Close or capability revocation.

The existing fresh protected proof and network-deny preview latch remain.
Provider simulation uses raw developer mode, not real OAuth. Ordinary OAuth
ignores the optional developer introduction builder. No real account, durable
member draft, entitlement or credit is created by this rehearsal. This

### As-built navigation and safety

The shared Home tools lead with a separate **Home** category/action on
Entry/onboarding, Seeker and Guardian toggles. Existing login restart, reset
and account-switch controls remain separate and unchanged. Home is disabled
while the shared tools are busy. The destination is the existing protected
**Developer Access** screen with **Seeker / Guardian / Onboarding**.

New `lib/dev/zyra_dev_home.dart` captures the root Navigator before refreshing
the protected Founder proof. Refresh can retract/dispose the initiating
toggle, so its old context is not used after the wait. The helper checks the
retained navigator, account generation, signing-out state, current route,
fresh capability and any owning-flow guard. Duplicate pending dispatch is
rejected. A current landing is reused; otherwise a fresh protected landing
is pushed with only the first root retained. Home does not await the
destination route's lifetime.

Onboarding's rendered Home binds to the exact registration. Its owning State
checks mounted/isolation/current route/local generation plus an in-progress
guard. Generic Home rejects any registered onboarding owner both before and
after proof; a stale captured-null toggle cannot remove a new/covered flow.
This absence-owner edge case was identified in internal read-only review,
fixed and traced to OA257. Both role callbacks reject disposed callers.

Home does not sign out, reset the account/sample, invoke providers, release
the raw network-deny latch, grant entitlements or spend credits. Child screens
still own their safe insets. Ordinary member navigation/authentication and
production onboarding design remain unchanged.

## 14. 2026-09-29 full-journey direction continuity — local preview only

### Founder request and source correction

After the §13 delivery, the Founder observed that each Mobbin-inspired option
had a distinctive opening but the subsequent onboarding pages felt like the
existing app. He requested a check of all ten directions, a coherent theme
through the real-feeling initial journey, and a local app update. This section
does not adopt any direction as the production default. The ordinary member
flow and **Current** comparator retain their existing visual treatment. The
common Apple/Google Welcome remains the same for every choice, as previously
directed; the ten distinct three-slide stories then carry their composition
into role choice and the local Seeker/Guardian initial setup sequence.

The preview Navigator in `SOURCE/lib/dev/dev_signup_process.dart` now supplies
a session-local `OnboardingPreviewStyle` scope keyed by the selected direction.
`current` deliberately resolves to no preview style. The neutral widget in
`SOURCE/lib/widgets/zyra_onboarding_direction_style.dart` is independent of
the developer enum; production screens consume it only when their existing
`devPreview` branch is active. It centralises direction-specific alignment,
spacing, card and button geometry, decorative motif and progress presentation.
No route, step count, question, answer, validation, permission request or
completion rule is changed by a visual token. The decorative Canvas motifs
have no interaction or accessibility meaning.

The current source consumers are role selection, Seeker and Guardian flow
containers, the shared onboarding scaffold/top bar, stage intro, account-path
overview, IDV, transition and Guardian code surfaces. This gives the selected
direction a continuous visual language through the local initial journey,
without replacing the governed content or production registration. The ten
compositions remain: Structured timeline; Luma centred glow; Revolut layered
cards; Headspace organic calm; Mozi circles/people; Wise clear path; Uber bold
trust geometry; Ahead gentle orbit; How We Feel expressive mosaic; and Atoms
dotted/orbital progress. They retain ZYRA's Midnight Plum, gold, Poppins and
Syne; the §2 Mobbin references informed composition only. No external artwork
or screenshot asset was copied.

An internal visual review found the initial Wise/Uber card tint too pale for
white labels; the shared style uses a dark-plum, high-contrast card instead.
It also found centred direction titles could remain visually start-aligned
inside an intrinsic-width column; role choice now gives those text blocks full
available width before centring them. Compact text/layout remains part of the
visual regression gate. These are visual corrections in the protected preview,
not changes to live scoring, identity or account state.

**44/44** against the same pixels. The candidate-only folder

not a permission to remove real identity verification or write new backend
policy. The Seeker product route still finalises only after the existing
server-verified IDV condition. It then presents **Continue profile setup**
or **Go to My ZYRA · finish later**, with explicit hidden-profile and locked
Discovery/Journey consequences. Both live choice actions are bound to the
same authenticated signup owner and fail closed after account switching.
The Seeker's existing server-backed My ZYRA setup signal/resume route remains
the live source of truth. Guardian has no deferred multi-chapter setup card;
its ordinary IDV and completion route are unchanged.

In the protected local Founder rehearsal, `Skip · DEV` now advances Welcome,

## 19. 2026-09-30 lighter onboarding intro swipe and local delivery

### Founder feedback and exact as-built boundary

The Founder reported that the onboarding scroll felt too heavy and a normal
swipe often did not move the page. The protected arrow-mode visual story and
Current comparator intro previously required a fresh drag at the content
edge plus **50px of iOS-damped overscroll**. Nested vertical scrollables and
large-drag tests concealed how resistant that felt on a phone. This section
supersedes §17's 50px gesture description; §17 remains delivery history.

`SOURCE/lib/widgets/zyra_onboarding_edge_swipe.dart` now observes actual
finger travel on release, without taking the inner content scroll away. A
vertical-dominant **56px finger swipe** can advance one page, or go back,
when it starts at the relevant edge. Substantial overflow must first be read
to within 10px of its end; a page with only 40px or less of total overflow
behaves like a fitted page. A drag starting earlier in substantial content
scrolls that content and cannot simultaneously advance. Multitouch and
horizontal gestures do not trigger the action. The story has a pending-page
guard so one drag cannot skip multiple slides. Current intro preserves its
one-shot async completion/retry/route guards.

Live readers are `SOURCE/lib/dev/dev_onboarding_lab.dart` (the ten visual
three-slide studies), `SOURCE/lib/widgets/zyra_onboarding_study.dart` (the
inner scroll controller) and
`SOURCE/lib/screens/onboarding/onboarding_stage_intro_screen.dart` (Current
welcome/stage intro). The protected Atoms default, eleven style choices,
role selection, arrow tap fallback and developer access are unchanged.
Question and form pages are deliberately **not** submitted by swipe: their
valid-answer action remains intentional. Ordinary public signup is outside
this preview-only gesture change. No backend, persistence, provider,
account, permission, SQL or migration behavior changed.

### Verification and residual boundary

The initial focused three-suite gate passed **98/98**. A wider thirteen-suite
run exposed an older developer-access test that still expected role choice
directly after Google, inconsistent with the already-delivered Atoms default.
That test now traverses all three Atoms slides for Seeker and Guardian; its
single suite passed **15/15**. The final thirteen-suite onboarding,
navigation, role, access and no-heart regression passed **276/276**, and the
real-font visual layout suite passed **4/4** without replacing approved
goldens. New tests use an 82px Atoms/Current swipe, reverse movement, a
280px gesture that advances only one slide, compact overflow-first reading
and the final role handoff. `dart analyze` and `dart format` on all seven
changed Dart files passed with zero issues/changes.

## 27. 2026-09-30 four-screen protected introduction — OA-ZYRA-257

### Authority, scope and exact result

The Founder approved a named UI-only execution relay for the three intro
screens to become four, with the existing design, palette, typography,
navigation, developer Skip and ordinary member signup boundaries preserved.
All **ten themed** protected introductions now share the approved four-screen
wording below; their existing direction-specific art and composition on the
first three pages remain. Atoms remains the protected default. The separate
**Current Seeker onboarding** comparator still uses its existing two-page
`OnboardingStageIntroScreen` route and was not silently redefined. No
canonical onboarding question, provider, profile draft, role choice, live
Journey or database rule changed.

| Page | Label | Headline | Supporting / quotation content | Closing / explanation |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1/4 | WELCOME TO ZYRA | A first look. / A deeper understanding. | Attraction may begin the interest. Deen, character and shared values help guide what comes next. | A considered start. Not a race. |
| 2/4 | WHAT MATTERS | Private answers. / Clearer compatibility. | Understand meaningful areas of alignment without exposing each other’s private answers or preferences. | Your private answers stay private. |
| 3/4 | TRUSTED SUPPORT | Move forward with clarity, / consent and support. | Bring trusted Guardians into the right parts of your Journey while keeping personal boundaries clear. | Thoughtful choices, with trusted support. |
| 4/4 | A PRINCIPLE BEHIND ZYRA | Attraction matters. / Deen guides the decision. | Translated excerpt from the later report, attributed below. | Attraction can begin the interest. Deen, character and compatibility help guide the decision. |

The fourth page displays this shortened, faithful English translation of
the opening part: “When a man seeks to marry a woman, he asks first about
her beauty. If it is spoken well of, he asks about her religion. If that
too is spoken well of, he marries her; otherwise, his refusal is for the
sake of religion.” The visible attribution is: “Reported from Imam Ahmad
in al-Mardawi’s al-Insaf, 8:19.” The ZYRA explanation remains a separate
sentence beneath the quotation card. The card explicitly says **TRANSLATED
EXCERPT**; it is not a hadith, a direct manuscript claim, a legal ruling, or
a compatibility-score claim.

### Source research and qualification

The original Arabic was checked against two digital presentations of
al-Mardawi’s *al-Insaf fi Ma'rifat al-Rajih min al-Khilaf*, **Kitab al-Nikah**
(Book of Marriage), in the “two benefits” passage on **volume 8, page 19**:
[IslamWeb Arabic text](https://www.islamweb.net/ar/library/content/26/3927/كتاب-النكاح)
and [Ahlulbayt Library edition page](https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/8053/19).
The latter identifies the Muhammad Hamid al-Fiqi edition published by Dar
Ihya al-Turath al-Arabi. The verified opening and religious-decision clause
reads: “إذا خطب رجل امرأة سأل عن جمالها أولا فإن حمد سأل عن دينها فإن حمد
تزوج وإن لم يحمد يكون رده لأجل الدين”. The rest of the original explains
the concern about first praising religious commitment but then rejecting a
prospective wife for appearance; the screen excerpt preserves the point
that religious commitment is the final criterion in this reported example.

Al-Mardawi introduces the passage as Imam Ahmad’s saying, but he is a later
source; an independent first-hand statement or transmission chain was not
established in this UI research. Consequently the product says **Reported
from Imam Ahmad in al-Mardawi’s al-Insaf**, not “Imam Ahmad said” as an
unqualified direct quotation. The founder-supplied social graphic was used
only to understand the request. Its “vol. 17, part 8” citation was not
corroborated for the verified edition. The fuller graphic translation is
substantially aligned with the Arabic’s argument, but the app uses its own
careful excerpt rather than copying the graphic. The passage’s discussion
is about a man considering a prospective wife in a specific marriage-law
context; ZYRA’s separate gender-neutral explanation is an application of
the broad idea, not a claim that the historical quote itself was phrased
that way.

### As-built and verification

`SOURCE/lib/widgets/zyra_onboarding_study.dart` owns the approved copy,
four-page count and readable scrollable quote page. The three existing
direction-specific artworks are preserved, with Atoms ring progress now
divided into quarters; the fourth page uses the same plum/gold typography
and a compact quote emblem/card. `SOURCE/lib/dev/dev_onboarding_lab.dart`
owns four dots, `1 / 4`–`4 / 4`, the final-page Finish state and the
unchanged arrow/swipe/Skip actions. At the compact 320-point width, its
footer side slots became 44 and 52 points, leaving exactly 176 points for
four **44-point** dot targets. The transition picker’s explanatory text
and comment were corrected from three to four pages; the six motion
choices and default were not changed.

VoiceOver receives the excerpt, qualified attribution and citation in one
semantic unit, while the decorative emblem is excluded from speech. The
four dots announce their selected state and page number. All story pages
remain vertically readable by scrolling independently of the arrow/swipe
action. Real-font visual checks covered 390×844 at normal and 1.3× text,
320×568 at 1.4× text across all ten themed directions, and the Atoms quote
at 320×568 with 2.0× text. Heading, quotation, attribution, explanation
and footer action remained reachable, with no compact overflow after the
footer correction.

The final ten-suite **serial** Flutter test run passed **285/285**, covering
all direction flows, arrow and reverse swipe, transition styles, Seeker and

The deterministic UI gate report

## 29. 2026-09-30 Card Tilt redesign and protected default — OA-ZYRA-257

### Founder direction and as-built scope

The Founder liked the **Card tilt** concept but found its prior 380ms linear
motion too quick and two-dimensional, and chose it as the default for now.
The protected **Intro page transition** setting now defaults to Card tilt
in both the standalone visual lab and the continuous developer onboarding
rehearsal. Current slide, Quiet fade, Soft lift, Side glide and Focus zoom
remain selectable in the toggle. A change remains Founder-authorised,
session-local and reset on account generation. This does **not** replace the
ordinary member's separate production onboarding motion or alter a real
signup answer, profile, Auth path or route.

Card tilt now uses a **760ms ease-in-out sine** page animation. At rest the
story page is unchanged. During movement the outgoing and incoming stories
each become bounded, inset purple/gold cards with mild X-axis perspective
on the default vertical down-arrow path, or Y-axis perspective in the
horizontal comparison mode. They scale and move into separate portions of
the viewport: neither card nor its headline is sliced by the screen edge,
the two readable stories do not overlap, and the midpoint retains two
visible cards rather than a blank purple frame. Their shadow, border and
content emphasis vary continuously with the page position. Arrow taps,
forward/reverse swipes and the native pager still share the same selected
motion and one-page settlement. Reduced-motion mode continues to jump
immediately without these visual effects.

The first implementation review exposed an empty midpoint and flat effect.
Subsequent candidate checks exposed overlapping headlines and clipped
full-height panels. Those intermediate designs were not delivered. The
final 13 candidate-only frames under
`SOURCE/test/visual/candidates/onboarding_card_tilt_redesign_2026_09_30/`
show forward and reverse early/mid/late states at 390×844 without those
defects. The older 48 comparison images remain preserved; no approved
golden was replaced. The final treatment reads as a card-deck depth motion
with smaller cards at the midpoint. This is an internal visual check, not
the Founder's physical-device feel acceptance.

## 30. 2026-09-30 final Founder-relayed four-screen introduction copy — OA-ZYRA-257

### Authority, result and boundary

The Founder relayed and approved ChatGPT's final four-screen introduction
wording in conversation. This is a bounded replacement of the ten protected
themed introduction comparisons. It supersedes the copy table in §27, not
the four-page architecture, the separate Current Seeker comparator, the
Card Tilt motion/default (§29), the animated-arrow navigation default,
developer skip or the ordinary member signup flow. No backend, database,
migration, credit, Guardian permission or real account state was changed.
The direct instruction is one-off authority for this preview copy; it does
not resolve the Seeker Summary's open Compatibility dependencies or adopt
this introduction as live ordinary onboarding.

| Page | Label | Headline | Main text | Closing |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1/4 | MARRIAGE-FIRST | Start with attraction. / Then look deeper. | Learn about deen, values and marriage goals before deciding whether to take the next step. | Built for Muslims serious about marriage. |
| 2/4 | COMPATIBILITY | Understand where you align / before moving forward. | ZYRA shows where your values and expectations align, where they differ and what may need a conversation. | You see the compatibility result—not each other’s private answers. |
| 3/4 | GUARDIAN SUPPORT | Your Guardian stays / part of the Journey. | Your Guardian will support the important stages and can read your private conversations, helping you make informed decisions. | Trusted support, built into ZYRA. |
| 4/4 | A PRINCIPLE BEHIND ZYRA | It is reported from / Imam Ahmad: | TRANSLATED EXCERPT card; source credit below | A principle that helped shape ZYRA. |

Page 4's visible excerpt is an original shortened translation of the first
part: “When a man seeks a woman’s hand, he asks first about her beauty. If it
is spoken well of, he asks about her religion. If that too is spoken well of,
he marries her; otherwise, his refusal is for the sake of religion.” Its
visible source credit reads: “Reported from Imam Ahmad (may Allah have mercy
on him) by al-Mardawi in al-Insaf, 8:19.” The honorific is in English because
the reading font used by the visual test does not contain the Arabic glyphs;
the direct Arabic version rendered as missing-glyph boxes in candidate
review. The page headline is therefore the same cautious report attribution
without an unreadable glyph sequence.

### Source and interpretation check

The Arabic anchor is “إذا خطب رجل امرأة سأل عن جمالها أولا . فإن حمد : سأل
عن دينها . فإن حمد : تزوج”. The remaining source passage explains that an
unfavourable religious assessment would be the reason to decline; it contrasts
this with first checking religion and then declining on appearance. We
verified al-Mardawi's *al-Insaf fi Ma'rifat al-Rajih min al-Khilaf*, Book of
Marriage, **volume 8, page 19**, in the
[Islamweb text](https://www.islamweb.net/ar/library/content/26/3927/كتاب-النكاح),
and checked the interpretive point against
[Islamweb's scholarly discussion](https://www.islamweb.net/amp/ar/fatwa/298237/).
Al-Mardawi attributes the passage to Imam Ahmad, but no contemporaneous
transmission chain or direct Ahmad-era wording was independently established.
The screen accordingly says “It is reported from”, identifies its card as
a translated **excerpt**, and does not present the report as Qur'an, hadith,
a guaranteed legal ruling, or the only Islamic way to choose a spouse.
The excerpt does not show the full inverse-case explanation; the citation
and this handover preserve that context.

The Guardian sentence is approved introductory capability wording, not a
complete current-access disclosure. The existing Journey-start disclosure
still explains eligible Guardians on both sides, and Main/Additional access
remains controlled by Journey state, membership, Seeker permission and
safety rules. OA-ZYRA-296 continues to track separate live View/Chat
enforcement. No Guardian-access code or policy was changed here.

## 31. 2026-09-30 progressive introduction and connected motion — OA-ZYRA-257

### Founder direction and as-built interaction

The Founder asked for the four opening cards to reward reading before
advancing: no down arrow on cards 1–3; touch-to-reveal copy, right swipe for
the next card and left swipe for the previous card; a down arrow to continue
only on card 4. The protected comparison and the continuous local onboarding
rehearsal now share that behaviour across all ten themed directions. Each
card begins with its artwork/label, then a tap reveals the heading, another
the main text, and another the closing line. Each new group fades/rises into
place over 620ms without moving the remaining layout. A right swipe cannot
cross an unread card; a left swipe returns immediately. The fourth card's
arrow appears only after its final line is revealed, and its existing upward
finger gesture can then continue downward in the onboarding sequence. Page
dots cannot jump over unread content. The separate **Skip · DEV** remains an
explicit protected bypass; the alternate Continue-button setting remains
available and steps through the same reveal gate. The requested right-to-next
and left-to-previous gesture is deliberate, including the reversed visual
geometry for Side glide. No page-count, wording, authority or account-state
contract changed.

The selected **Guided paths** role card now expands in place with a 480ms
ease-in-out transition, while the unselected card fades and collapses; the
rail, text, border, selection mark and continuation cue move together. A
second tap restores both choices. This role-choice treatment also applies to
the ordinary role screen, without changing the Seeker/Guardian selection or
save action. Under reduced motion or accessible navigation, the selection
changes immediately, without retaining the outgoing card for a frame.

On the protected Atoms **Welcome to ZYRA** page, the motif, greeting,
“Together, we’ll build four things” line, and roadmap rows 1–4 enter in
sequence over 2.9s with restrained fade/8px lift. A seven-pixel gold dot in
the motif connects this otherwise separate page to the later gold progress
marks, without adding a ring or larger card. The ordinary member Welcome
page does not receive this preview-only entrance or cue. Screen-reader
navigation exposes the full intro copy immediately; Welcome content is also
immediate for reduced-motion and accessible-navigation users. The protected
developer capability, real signup actions and existing skip/finish routes
remain bounded as before.

## 38. 2026-10-01 onboarding progress semantics and count alignment — OA-ZYRA-257 / OA-ZYRA-286

The Founder compared three consecutive screens: role choice said “1 of 2”
beside a seven-cell rail, Welcome then looked unnumbered, and the following
screen said “1 of 4” beside a different gold-cell count. These were
contradictory cues, not seven or two sequential account steps. The correction
separates branch choice, overview, account prerequisites, the four-stage
profile journey and fractional question progress. It preserves the approved
ZYRA palette, type, role selection, navigation, motion and protected-preview
boundary.

The role-choice screen, in the ordinary route and protected comparison, now
labels its header **CHOOSE YOUR PATH**. Choosing Seeker or Guardian is an
unnumbered branch, so its fabricated “ACCOUNT SETUP · 1 OF 2” label and
half-complete rail are gone. The previous rail height is reserved to avoid a
layout jump. The Welcome screen says **YOUR SIGN-UP JOURNEY · OVERVIEW** and
no longer shows unexplained two-page footer dots; its action is **Continue to
Account Essentials**. The first intro is genuinely before the three account
questions and identity check, so it is an unnumbered **ACCOUNT ESSENTIALS**
intro describing first name, gender and date of birth, not “Stage 1 of 4”.
The four-stage profile journey begins after that prerequisite flow. Later
stage intros label **PROFILE JOURNEY** and **STAGE x OF 4**, and render
exactly four segments in every preview direction and ordinary styling.
Footer two-page dots were also removed from the stage intro; they represented
local Welcome/intro page mechanics, not the user's overall journey.

The shared `OnboardingPreviewProgress` now distinguishes `steps` from
fractional progress. Stage intros use the exact step count; chapter/question
fractions use a continuous rail rather than direction-dependent decorative
three-, four-, five- or seven-cell rows. Its optional semantic label lets a
caller announce a named question or section while retaining the numeric
percent value for assistive technology. The four-stage rail has one explicit
“Stage x of 4” semantic value. The Atoms artwork's independent gold motif is
decorative and is not a count or completion claim.

The correction extends through the actual onboarding route, not only the
three screenshots. Seeker Account Essentials says `QUESTION x OF 3`, Journey
Bio says `STEP x OF 5`, Guardian setup says `QUESTION x OF 4`, and shared
question headers name the current local scope. Compatibility Part 1's rail
advances across its 11 sections rather than resetting inside each section;
Part 2 explicitly says `SECTION x OF 11`. The conditional Profile flow counts
only active questions after Article 9 consent and explicit no-preference
branches. These labels describe the current question/section position, not a
claim that the visible unanswered question is complete. The final-question
screens no longer opt into a premature completion colour. Seeker roadmap
marks Account Essentials as a completed prerequisite and numbers only its
four subsequent stages; Guardian roadmap items no longer masquerade as a
page denominator. The fictional code orientation no longer claims 100%
onboarding completion. No persistence, scoring, verification or account
transition contract was changed for this progress presentation.

- Shared visual contract: `SOURCE/lib/widgets/zyra_onboarding_direction_style.dart`,
  `SOURCE/lib/widgets/onboarding_top_bar.dart`,
  `SOURCE/lib/widgets/onboarding_scaffold.dart`.
- Branch, intro and roadmap: `SOURCE/lib/screens/auth/role_selection_screen.dart`,
  `SOURCE/lib/screens/onboarding/onboarding_stage_intro_screen.dart`,
  `SOURCE/lib/screens/onboarding/account_path_overview_screen.dart`.
- Question and orientation flows: `SOURCE/lib/screens/onboarding/onboarding_flow.dart`,

The Founder supplied two screenshots of the protected opening story's third
card. Before and after the staged text reveal, its footer printed either
“Tap the page to continue reading” or “Swipe right for the next page.” He
requested removal of these visible interaction instructions across the four
opening cards and made the broader design intent explicit: describing a
gesture or implementation requirement to Codex does **not** automatically
authorise explanatory text in the app. That correction supersedes §33's
later-card guidance and §34's first-card-only prompt removal as current
presentation facts; those sections remain preserved as dated build history.

fixed-height footer clear while their copy reveals. The page dots, page
count, bottom-right **Skip · DEV**, and final card's gold down arrow remain.

follow the same reveal gate, leftward swipes return, and card four finishes

Continue-button comparison retains its labelled button. The visible caption
is removed, not the gestures or screen-reader-only action hints. No account,
provider, draft, identity, permission or backend path changed.

Forward UX copy rule from this direct Founder instruction: an interaction or
implementation instruction given to the builder is **not** user-facing UI
copy by default. Add visible gesture guidance when the Founder requests it
or a clear, demonstrated usability need warrants it; keep necessary accessibility
labels, validation feedback and meaningful action labels. This is a scoped
presentation rule, not a redesign of all onboarding prose.

## 42. 2026-10-01 welcoming prelude and two coherent opening-story navigation choices — OA-ZYRA-257 / OA-ZYRA-286

The Founder found the quote-only first frame too abrupt and directly asked for
an earlier welcome, an automatic three-line sequence, a pulsing gold dot, and
two selectable ways to move through the subsequent introduction. The ten
protected themed onboarding rehearsals now begin with an **unnumbered**
welcome: “Welcome to ZYRA, [first name].” (the protected preview uses its
current profile first name or the sample name Shakeel), then “We pray Allah
makes this journey easy for you and your family.”, then “Let’s begin.” The
first two changes occur automatically at approximately 1.7-second intervals.
The last line remains until a deliberate screen tap; its gold dot pulses for
four bounded cycles and it alone shows the explicitly requested “Tap screen to
continue” hint. Reduced-motion/accessibility navigation shows the final line
immediately without making the user wait through timed motion. `Skip · DEV`

## 61. 2026-10-01 Two gold-animation comparisons — OA-ZYRA-257 / OA-ZYRA-286

remains the selected design default. Both controls are session-local and
account-generation scoped. No ordinary member sign-in/onboarding, stored
preference, authentication or backend behaviour changed.

each opening story card; card text reveals within the same page do not restart

up to 7 px on a 4,800 ms cycle. Neither mode changes horizontal position or
radius, so Diagonal edge's full-halo 24 px text clearance from §60 remains
true throughout the animation. Both modes settle to a static pattern when
the device requests reduced motion or accessible navigation. Motion mode
changes can be previewed immediately from the toggle and do not restart
onboarding. The other ten gold-design comparisons remain available.

## 89. 2026-10-02 Full progressive onboarding review and cleanup — OA-ZYRA-303

### Authority, current flow and preserved decisions

The Founder directly requested a deep review and implementation against the
current intended model, identifying old and new onboarding mixed together.
This authorizes reversible client cleanup, tests, documentation and the local
phone update, not production schema/security/provider changes. Only the Founder
accepts the result; Codex's builder checks are internal verification.

| Boundary | Current client behavior |
| --- | --- |
| Account admission | Role choice, truthful account orientation, essentials, mandatory real identity verification, confirmed account finalization. No four-profile roadmap yet. |
| Verified Seeker choice | “Your account is ready.” Two labelled actions: Continue profile setup, or Go to My ZYRA · finish later. Entering My ZYRA does not publish or unlock Discovery/Journeys. |
| Profile overview | After IDV, four deliberate card reveals: Your Profile, optional Journey Bio, Compatibility, Review & go live. Arrow appears after the fourth. |
| Section work | Concise handoff, actual questions, local question progress. Profile/Bio/Compatibility have a real Finish later action. Mandatory essentials and IDV do not. |
| Resume | Re-read own saved fields and choose first unfinished section. A partial preferences row is not a finished profile; basic_profile is a publication flag, not a chapter checkpoint. |
| Final review | Read actual profile completeness, server question progress and IDV; explicit Go live rechecks and confirms the existing guarded RPC outcome. Arrival is not publication. |
| Guardian | Actual account essentials including relationship, IDV and responsibilities. No fictional photos/Bio/membership roadmap and no assumption of family relationship. |

The protected opening story is preserved: named welcome, praise/blessings,
prayer (local 1/3–3/3); photo-first, Compatibility, Guardian (local 1/3–3/3);
then the named final onboarding handoff. The prayer arrow reading delay,
section-only physical vertical scroll, unified selected card content motion,
same-page Imam Ahmad quotation/photo explanation with qualified attribution
but no bibliography, Poppins text, gold Syne wordmark, accepted Luma direction,
live original diagonal dust and no unsolicited gesture-caption text remain.
No extra introduction page, new scorer or subscription claim was inserted.

### Findings corrected in source

- Separate AccountEssentialsIntroScreen replaces the premature four-stage
  roadmap before name/gender/DOB/IDV. The four-profile orientation belongs only
  after admission or when resuming its first section.
- Shared effectiveStyleOf provides Luma presentation to ordinary questions and
  protected previews without developer authorization. Explicit Current remains
  a comparison. Gold wordmarks/arrows, truthful section labels and local counts
  now cover both roles; help uses actual question/privacy text and a functional
  Close action, not fabricated/dead controls.
- Profile resume validates saved photos (three), location, details/preferences
  and explicit consent state. Optional reviewed empty Bio persists bio_reviewed
  in existing answer metadata; legacy compatibility work implies Bio already
  passed. No database field or policy migration was introduced.
- Own-account strict reads distinguish absent drafts from failed reads and
  failed/malformed consent evidence. Ten-second restore timeouts lead to retry,
  not an empty writable form. Photo URL/read failures remain failures.
- Profile, Bio and Compatibility await confirmed own-row saves before each
  answer advance. Failed saves retain data and retry; partial Finish later saves
  only valid draft fields, rechecks real IDV and opens My ZYRA without publishing.
- Captured owner/account generation, sticky preview mode, current-route checks
  and one-shot latches protect saves, resume, account choice and all section
  handoffs against signout, A→B→A changes and repeated callbacks.
- Final review replaces false awaiting-IDV/completion copy with loaded truth.
  Explicit publication is preflighted and reloaded; unknown outcome requires
  refresh rather than another uncontrolled write. The compatibility editor now
  restores/saves for the captured principal and never becomes live after DEV
  revocation. DOB/gender copy correctly points to account essentials.
- Reduced-motion PageView uses jumpToPage, avoiding Flutter's zero-duration
  animateToPage assertion. Compact and large-text controls remain reachable;
  roadmap notes wrap rather than truncating, Guardian status stacks on narrow
  screens, and the optional app-entry action follows the gold theme.

Main changed sources: onboarding_stage_intro_screen, onboarding_flow,
account_path_overview_screen, chapter2_flow, bio_onboarding_flow,
values_preferences_flow, profile_ready_screen, seeker_setup_navigation,
home_shell, wali_onboarding_flow, role_selection_screen, transition_screen,
identity_verification_screen, edit_profile_screen; shared scaffold/top-bar/style/
arrow widgets; journey_bio and seeker_setup_checkpoint models; own answers,
details, photos, profile and consent services. New mock regressions cover current
journey/checkpoints, consent restore, handoff ownership and editor ownership.
Candidate-only real-font visual harnesses cover current account/choice/roadmap/
handoff/Guardian and ready/incomplete/live review at standard and compact
large-text sizes. Existing approved golden files are untouched.

### Verification, delivery and unresolved boundaries

Final affected behavioral run: **613 passed, one skipped, zero failed** across
46 suites. The default-off IDV-fuse test skipped by configuration was separately
run with the flag enabled against a mock transport: **one passed**; the installed
artifact's flag remains default-off. Scoped analysis of 73 items found no issues.
Twelve real-font visual checks passed, including 320×568 at 1.4× text; only
candidate captures were produced, not approved-golden promotion.

The production-flavour PROFILE build (1.0.0+5, Founder-only compile capability)
passed strict/deep signature verification, installed and native-launched on the
paired iPhone 15 Pro Max on 2026-10-02. Source hashes rechecked after delivery
match the verified build inputs. Runner SHA-256:
`a0c1a5cc8c098d0c068c140527c28c55b20e850d1b43fa5a15f7f31bd27768e3`;
Dart AOT SHA-256:
`2787da5f5448ef6b2cc524863a2c840114fb20c3531db405793f1300828157c9`.
Native process launch is not live provider end-to-end proof or Founder on-screen

Open backend/product gates, each traced by OA-ZYRA-303 and linked existing OAs:

1. OA-ZYRA-266: live IDV provider remains unconfigured and held existing-account

2. OA-ZYRA-193/303: explicit Article9 refusal skips sensitive questions, but

3. OA-ZYRA-193/303: server's older minimum-one-photo publication check differs

4. OA-ZYRA-257/286/303: Founder physical flow/motion acceptance and future

## 90. 2026-10-02 Required public names and open name-entry design — DR-ZYRA-251 / OA-ZYRA-145

### Exact Founder request and unchanged flow

The Founder disliked the filled name-entry block and asked about Male/Female
and last name. On being asked surname visibility, the Founder explicitly ruled
that last name must not be optional and must appear publicly. This is the GO
for the bounded client update and its local delivery, not database/provider
activation. DR-ZYRA-251 records the public/required decision; OA-ZYRA-145 already
owns surname capture and is reused rather than opening a duplicate finding.

Both Seeker and Guardian name questions now say “What's your name?” with the
disclosure “Both names will appear on your public profile.” First name and last
name are two required inputs on the same question, so account question counts
remain three for Seeker and four for Guardian. Seeker Male/Female remains
question 2, then DOB; Guardian DOB remains question 2 and Male/Female question
3, then relationship. DOB remains private. No gender policy, opening story,
motion, stage order, compatibility scoring or four-part profile question was
changed by this follow-up.

The shared name field uses centred Poppins, open gold underlines, gold cursor,
given/family-name autofill and a 60-character limit. It opts out of the selected
style's filled answer card, not the rest of the scaffold. Keyboard Next moves
once to last name; Done dismisses only and cannot skip a question. The gold
continue action requires both trimmed names; a first name alone or whitespace
surname is insufficient. Standard/compact large-text and keyboard layouts are
checked; compact content can scroll, and the fixed action remains reachable.
The gold Syne ZYRA wordmark stays separate from ordinary reading text.

### Persistence, recovery and identity boundaries

- New protected draft payload v2 saves both names using the same opaque secure
  v1 storage key, avoiding orphaned recovery. Valid old v1 drafts missing last
  name return to question 1, retaining gender/DOB/relationship rather than
  inventing a surname from provider display names. Invalid legacy values are
  rejected before migration. Even a partially created profile cannot erase an
  unfinished name draft or bypass that recovery on cold start.
- Real server-verified IDV still precedes account finalization. The existing
  profile creation RPC is retained, followed by an UPDATE of only the existing
  public profiles.surname_line column, pinned to captured owner, role, session
  and account generation. Zero rows, wrong returned values, errors, preview or
  stale owners are not confirmed saves. These transport checks are mock tests,
  not live production probes.
- Guardian's existing completion/publication helper is deferred until surname
  confirmation in protected signup. Final own-row verification checks id, role,
  first_name and surname_line before confirmed local draft erasure/admission.
  Failures retain recovery for retry. Writes are not an atomic new database
  transaction, and already-dispatched server/native operations cannot be
  claimed rolled back by a later client guard.
- Draft read/save/clear/sign-out capture generation before queueing, then check
  it at each storage boundary. A→B→A cannot release an old queued erase/write,
  old draft result or logout into a newly authenticated same-ID session. The
  dedicated sign-out latch and intentional beginSignOut ordering are retained.
- Local AppState carries surname for existing full-name profile presentation;
  a personalised welcome continues to use first name only. DEV skip supplies
  local preview values only, never writes member surname or grants identity.

The existing surname column/grants/public rendering are repository evidence;
no migration was added or applied. This is new-signup client enforcement, not
a database-wide non-null requirement or automatic backfill/forced signup for
existing admitted accounts. Profile Settings editing is outside this request.
Public display remains subject to existing profile visibility permissions.

Changed production files: zyra_onboarding_name_field (new), shared scaffold,
initial_signup_draft model/repository/finalizer, profile service, session router,
AppState, Seeker/Guardian flows and account essentials introduction copy.
Regressions cover required/public fields, keyboard focus, same-device recovery,
legacy migration, real-IDV gating, surname confirmation/failure/retry,
Guardian ordering, zero-row refusal, cold start, account changes, DEV isolation
and the complete style/role/action matrix. The keyboard Next test caught and
fixed a double traversal rather than weakening its assertion.

## 94. 2026-10-03 Purpose-led opening review — DR-ZYRA-253 / OA-ZYRA-303/286

### Purpose and bounded Founder authority

The Founder asked for one deep review and implementation of a meaningful,
friendly, readable, clearly sectioned opening. Three full welcome/praise/prayer
clicks felt like gap-filling; typed-on text did not match the onboarding culture.
The review traced the current login/rehearsal boundary, opening narrative,
read controls, category copy, account orientation, role routes and current
DR-ZYRA-252 Membership → identity sequence. Two independent read-only audits
identified sparse opening pages, hidden useful explanations, repeated photo/
Compatibility wording, interruptible animations and accessibility differences.
This authorizes a presentation/narrative iteration, not new product policies,
promotion into ordinary OAuth routing or activation of any held service.

### As built and why each page exists

1. **Welcome / orientation:** named greeting when supplied, with the honest
   role-neutral purpose: a space to seek marriage or support someone's Journey.
   Praise/blessings and the exact prayer sit in a lighter reading hierarchy on
   that same page. The unchanged exact sentence states that identity
   verification is required to complete sign-up, not that every existing
   member has already passed a live provider. One complete page replaces
   three compulsory single-sentence pages.
2. **Photos / first impression:** retains “Match on attraction. Connect on
   deen.” The body concentrates on photos and mutual choice rather than
   repeating the next Compatibility category. An optional “Why photos first?”
   card opens the retained full reported Imam Ahmad excerpt on this same page,
   with “Reported from Imam Ahmad” but no visible bibliography. It is optional
   supporting context, not another required reveal, slide or swipe destination.
3. **Compatibility / understanding:** “Discover where you align.” explains
   deen, values and marriage expectations, common ground and conversation,
   while retaining the existing private-answer/result distinction. No score,
   availability, paid unlock or future calculation-policy promise is invented.
4. **Guardians / shared support:** preserves multiple-Guardian, together-
   Journey guidance without assuming family, printing intrusive blanket chat
   access or granting a relationship/Main authority. Its final down action
   goes straight into the existing “How will you use ZYRA first?” role choice.
   The redundant final Let's begin handoff is removed.

Full reading copy appears on entry. Welcome is unnumbered; How ZYRA works is
one accurate three-dot/count section, with prior/current dots gold. No visible
tap/swipe instruction is added. Back/DEV Skip remain. Within-theme page taps,
right/left gestures and a clear next chevron share the same action; section
boundaries use down actions. The explicit scroll-all comparison still works.

### Unified motion, lifecycle and accessibility

`DevOnboardingIntroduction` now delegates to the isolated
`dev_onboarding_opening.dart` reader. The old continuous/Let's begin branches
were removed from the standalone lab so four-card studies stay a separate
comparison rather than an alternate half-active signup narrative. Five
whole-page descent controls remain visible; old typewriter enum values stay
compatible for helper/comparator tests but cannot type this reader. Duration,
travel and Soft landing easing are honored; default remains Slow descent.

One foreground page holds icon and complete copy. At default 1350 ms the
outgoing content eases away for 35% of the time, is replaced only when fully
transparent, then the new content enters for 65%. There are never two reading
pages at once. Section travel is distinct from ordinary subtle page movement;
final route handoff exits over500 ms and uses the existing parent entry. The
canvas and live diagonal decoration remain fixed. Icon slots are140 px on
standard phones/120 px compact, independent of copy length. Poppins reading
text and the gold Syne wordmark remain unchanged.

Transitions are latched until fully settled. Welcome waits until its login
route/foreground entrance completes, then offers the repeating arrow after
three seconds; its swipe uses the same gate. Reduced-motion/accessibility
shows complete text and stationary controls without the delay. Read overflow
uses a clamped inner scroll; a long drag starting away from the reading edge
does not accidentally cross the section. Progress dots report state, not
fake disabled buttons. The artwork is excluded from accessibility reading.

Internal tests exposed two real implementation lifecycle bugs, both repaired:
the lazy controller could first create a ticker during denied-state disposal;
and a retained Guardian action could call setState after the old flow was
disposed. Eager controller initialization, mounted/persistent-denial checks,
canceled timers and awaited animation checks fix those cases. Revocation
cannot be undone by regrant in an old reader; only expected TickerCanceled is
caught, not unexpected animation failures. This is internal verification,
not a self-approved security/visual acceptance claim.

### Internal checks and exact local delivery

Final integrated15-suite invocation passed **363/363**, no failures/skips.
It covers both role routes, complete reading pages, three local dots,
same-page quote expansion, Back/Skip, rapid/stale callbacks, no overlap,
direction controls, fixed anchors/canvas, reduced/accessibility motion,
draft recovery, Membership intent and protected no-I/O/denial boundaries.

were not hidden: obsolete seven-page/15-typing-control expectations were
updated; the exact timer harness now crosses the ticker completion before

## 99. Standalone verification trust page

### Founder request and claim boundary

On 2026-10-04 the Founder requested that the Welcome sentence “Identity
verification is required to complete sign-up.” become a meaningful standalone
page communicating ZYRA's serious, verified-admission standard. DR-ZYRA-256
authorizes this protected local presentation amendment. It does not promote
the rehearsal into ordinary OAuth routing or permit claims about Muzz.

The local admission contract is supported by DR-ZYRA-221 and
`zyra_initial_signup_draft_service.dart` finalization: the current own-account
IDV state must allow admission. The provider remains unavailable and the
existing-account enforcement fuse remains held/default-off under OA-ZYRA-266.
Therefore the new copy states a joining requirement, not a measured fact that
every existing member is verified. Identity checks are not proof of intentions,
conduct, safety, background checks or profile-photo matching.

### As built

Welcome retains its supplied first name, role-neutral purpose, praise/blessings
and prayer. Only its verification sentence is removed. Our community follows
on its own page with the exact text:

- Heading: “Verified people. Serious intentions.”
- Body: “Every Seeker and Guardian must complete identity verification before
  joining ZYRA.”
- Closing: “A shared standard for a community focused on marriage.”

The new shield uses the existing gold halo and icon anchor. A private ordered
enum owns Welcome → verification → photos → Compatibility → Guardian. Existing
theme identities/keys remain 1–3; Welcome/trust are unnumbered and do not add
a theme dot. Back retraces photos → trust → Welcome → provider. Skip is local
and sequential; only the final Guardian action invokes the parent role route.

Welcome/trust each start their own three-second arrow delay after foreground
entrance. The timer captures the logical page and is cancelled on navigation,
revocation and disposal; reduced-motion/accessibility bypasses motion/delay.
Both surrounding handoffs reuse the existing section-scroll animation, one
clear-then-enter foreground tree and unchanged static canvas/live gold dust.
Trust cannot advance from a body tap/horizontal swipe while awaiting its down
checkpoint. Poppins, gold Syne wordmark, approved photos paragraph, optional
same-page reported Imam Ahmad quote, other theme copy and final role choice
are unchanged. Standalone direction comparisons retain their existing model.

### Exact source and evidence

Only two product files change:

- `SOURCE/lib/dev/dev_onboarding_opening.dart`: ordered checkpoint navigation,
  trust copy/keys, local progress and owned reading-delay timer.
- `SOURCE/lib/widgets/zyra_onboarding_study.dart`: optional intro symbol using
  the existing gold halo; repaint identity includes that symbol. Existing
  topic/direction artwork and standalone reader are otherwise unchanged.

Existing functional and candidate-only regression files are updated to visit
trust without altering their three-theme expectations. New real-font captures
live in `SOURCE/test/visual/candidates/verification_trust_2026_10_04/`; older
candidates and approved goldens are preserved. Internal results and exact
hashes are recorded in
`SYSTEM/RESOURCES/REVIEWS/ONBOARDING_VERIFICATION_TRUST_2026-10-04_VERIFICATION.json`.
The complete 225-file nonvisual selection excluding the separately rewritten
trust suite passed 2504 tests with two existing held-configuration skips and
zero failures (2m30s). The dedicated trust suite passed 20/20, so all 226
functional files are covered with 2524 passes and two skips. A final combined
trust/purpose-led real-font run passed 22/22. Earlier focused navigation smoke
passed 53/53; the four flow/authorization/CTA/transition files passed 196/196.
The full-source analyzer reported no issues (18.9s); the final 11-file format
check changed nothing. The trust suite's first three harness-only failures
(clock sampling and semantics-handle cleanup) were corrected in tests; the
final measured suites above passed without a product-code repair.

the full body and closing remain reachable above the footer. No approved

## 109. First Seeker photo before membership

On 2026-10-04 the Founder requests a minimum first photo before subscription
choice because Seekers can enter My ZYRA after verification and finish the
remaining profile later. DR-ZYRA-264 changes Seeker account setup to names,
gender, DOB and first photo, then Membership and mandatory identity check.
Guardian keeps its four identity/relationship questions and no Seeker-photo
requirement. The accepted opening, guided choice cards, Family/One comparison,
prices, arrows, fonts and palette are not redesigned.

### Implementation and data flow

The new account-photo question uses the existing question scaffold, Poppins,
gold/frost choice language and existing choose/crop/privacy-processor batch.
It requires one successfully staged photo and a confirmed secure draft before
Membership. Cancellation, processing/upload/save errors remain on that screen;
replacement save failure retains the previous confirmed selection. No automatic
advance follows choosing a photo. The four-question progress and Seeker account
orientation now include the first photo. Developer skip uses an in-memory
one-pixel fixture only, under existing protected preview authority.

Nine product sources change:

- `SOURCE/lib/screens/onboarding/onboarding_flow.dart`: required fourth question,
  busy/save locks, owner/generation checks, URL restoration and narrow recovery.
- `SOURCE/lib/widgets/zyra_signup_first_photo_card.dart`: photo/input presentation.
- `SOURCE/lib/screens/profile/zyra_photo_batch_flow.dart`: optional caller guard
  before/after picking, crop and each save; other callers retain defaults.
- `SOURCE/lib/models/zyra_initial_signup_draft.dart`: v4 tokenless owner-folder
  JPEG reference; older Seeker step3 resumes at photo with names/choice retained.
- `SOURCE/lib/services/zyra_initial_signup_draft_service.dart`: owner-bound
  persistence, pending-finalization marker and confirmed post-IDV photo attach.
- `SOURCE/lib/services/zyra_photo_service.dart`: pinned-backend/path validation,
  fresh signing, guarded processor/upload and nondestructive attach/readback.
- `SOURCE/lib/services/zyra_session_router.dart`: unfinished/marked same-device
  recovery before ordinary member routing, never a global historical-photo gate.
- `SOURCE/lib/screens/onboarding/chapter2_flow.dart`: preview carries the chosen
  photo; live restores actual saved rows; Add more photos copy when present.
- `SOURCE/lib/screens/onboarding/onboarding_stage_intro_screen.dart`: account
  orientation includes one photo before Membership; other stages remain.

Only the tokenless storage path enters native protected same-device recovery.
Photo bytes and signed URLs are never persisted in that draft. Existing upload
processing removes metadata and bounds the image; upload is not an identity,
photo-ownership or moderation verdict. Before private upload, the caller captures
account/epoch and rechecks across picker/crop/processor/upload gaps. Guardian
drafts reject a Seeker photo; repository validation rejects foreign-owner paths.

Finalization still fetches verified server identity before creating a profile.
It saves the commercial preference without activation, writes a minimal pending
marker, creates/confirms the profile and public surname, then signs/attaches/
reads back the first photo before confirmed draft and marker erasure. Attachment
does not delete or reorder existing photo rows, and refuses a seventh row.
Confirmed URLs are projected to AppState only after success. Private form data,
photo reference and membership intent are erased on sign-out; the secure
owner/backend-keyed `{version:1,pending:true}` protocol marker alone survives
until successful finalization. It is not identity or access proof.

Marker-only recovery starts the Seeker essentials again without guessing erased
identity answers. When a profile exists, own saved photos can be reused. At six
saved photos, it restores the saved main photo instead of a staged seventh and
does not offer another upload at signup. Failed recovery is retry-only, not
permission to assume zero photos; reads/signing are bounded to ten seconds.
Stale-owner/epoch/disposed callbacks cannot change the current form.

The later profile section still requires three photos (maximum six) and retains
its consent, completeness and server-confirmed publication rules. One early
photo does not set Chapter2/profile/publication complete or unlock Discovery/
Journeys. Existing admitted accounts are not backfilled or gated by this change.

### Evidence and limits

Read-only live metadata confirms a private profile-photos bucket, own-folder
INSERT/SELECT independent of a profile row, and profile_photos FK to profiles.
The bounded evidence reads no members, image bytes or object paths and changes
no live data/policy. It is not a real upload/sign/display test. Current SDK source
and primary Storage access-control documentation were checked; no package,
plugin, native signing, configuration or migration changed.

Final functional run:2672PASS/2existing skips/0fail across232nonvisual files,

visual review find no actionable clipping/overlap/unreachable action. Local
1px fixtures validate placement/scroll, not real photo/crop quality.

63.8s/56.1MB; deep strict signature/identity/existing provisioning profile
PASS. Fresh paired-phone checks remain locked; unlock requested. No install

## 110. Guardian signup requires one photo

On 2026-10-12 the Founder instructs that a Guardian signing up needs to upload
only one photo. This supersedes §109's Guardian no-photo clause, not its
Seeker requirements. Guardian essentials now ask for name, date of birth,
gender, relationship and one photo, followed by membership and identity
verification. There is no three-photo Guardian requirement and choosing a
photo does not automatically advance the question.

The existing choose, crop and privacy-processing flow is reused with a
one-photo picker allowance. A successful, current-owner selection and confirmed
protected draft are required before membership. Cancelled, empty, multiple,
foreign-owner or failed selections do not satisfy the question. Busy upload
blocks duplicate picking, forward and Back. Late results from a disposed,
covered, replaced-account or revoked preview cannot change the active form.
The shared photo card retains its Seeker defaults; Guardian copy states only
that one photo is needed and makes no new public/private audience promise.
The Guardian account overview and five-question progress match the new step.

Draft schema 5 allows an owner-folder JPEG reference for either role, never
photo bytes or a signed capability. Older Guardian schemas still reject injected
photo references; valid older membership checkpoints return to the photo
question with their answers and commercial preference retained. Seeker schema 4
photos and its four account questions remain unchanged. Protected previews use
an in-memory fixture and cannot persist a draft, upload or finalize a member.

After the verified server identity result and expected profile/surname readback,
both roles confirm the owned Storage reference and photo row before erasing
signup recovery. Guardian publication happens only after photo confirmation in
this client. An opaque owner/backend-bound Guardian pending marker is confirmed
before profile creation and cleared after successful completion. Explicit signout
erases form answers, photo reference and commercial preference but retains only
the retry marker. Marker-only recovery asks for erased essentials again, reuses
an existing saved photo where present and cannot invent identity answers.

The session router checks unfinished drafts and both role markers before ordinary
admission or remembered-role fallback. Conflicting markers, a draft/profile/marker
role mismatch, failed protected reads and owner/epoch changes fail closed.
Restored photo signing and own-photo reads are bounded to ten seconds; a failed
own-photo read is retry-only rather than permission to assume zero. A recovered
six-photo account reuses its saved main photo and does not add a seventh.

compile-fuse skip, zero failures, 128 seconds. That skipped identity-gate case

build keeps its existing rollout setting. Analysis is clean in nine seconds;
the 23-file format gate changes zero. Independent source and handover reviews
find no new client admission/recovery defect.

one-pixel fixture measures layout, not real portrait quality or native camera,
